                    return bin(Sub, l, (**y).clone(), sp);
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Sub,
                l: y,
                r: z,
            } = &r.kind
            {
                if as_int(z) == Some(0) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return bin(Sub, l, (**y).clone(), sp);
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Add,
                l: x,
                r: z,
            } = &l.kind
            {
                if as_int(z) == Some(0) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return bin(Sub, (**x).clone(), r, sp);
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Sub,
                l: x,
                r: z,
            } = &l.kind
            {
                if as_int(z) == Some(0) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return bin(Sub, (**x).clone(), r, sp);
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Sub,
                l: x,
                r: z,
            } = &r.kind
            {
                if as_int(z) == Some(0) && same(&l, x) && is_pure(&l, pure) && is_int_expr(&l) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return int(0);
                }
            }
        }
        Mul => {
            if let Some(c) = ri {
                if c.is_power_of_two() && c != 0 {
                    let sh = c.trailing_zeros() as u64;
                    *changed = true;
                    return bin(Shl, l, int(sh), sp);
                }
            }
            if let Some(c) = li {
                if c.is_power_of_two() && c != 0 {
                    let sh = c.trailing_zeros() as u64;
                    *changed = true;
                    return bin(Shl, r, int(sh), sp);
                }
            }
            if ri == Some(1) {
                *changed = true;
                return l;
            }
            if li == Some(1) {
                *changed = true;
                return r;
            }
            if ri == Some(0) && is_pure(&l, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return int(0);
            }
            if li == Some(0) && is_pure(&r, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return int(0);
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Mul,
                l: x,
                r: a,
            } = &l.kind
            {
                if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (as_int(a), ri) {
                    if ast_fold_mul(a, b).is_some() {
                        *changed = true;
                        return bin(Mul, (**x).clone(), int(a.wrapping_mul(b)), sp);
                    }
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Mul,
                l: a,
                r: x,
            } = &r.kind
            {
                if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (li, as_int(a)) {
                    if ast_fold_mul(a, b).is_some() {
                        *changed = true;
                        return bin(Mul, int(a.wrapping_mul(b)), (**x).clone(), sp);
                    }
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Mul,
                l: y,
                r: c,
            } = &r.kind
            {
                if as_int(c).is_some() {
                    *changed = true;
                    return bin(Mul, bin(Mul, l, (**y).clone(), sp), (**c).clone(), sp);
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Mul,
                l: x,
                r: c,
            } = &l.kind
            {
                if as_int(c).is_some() {
                    *changed = true;
                    return bin(Mul, bin(Mul, (**x).clone(), r, sp), (**c).clone(), sp);
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Add,
                l: x,
                r: a,
            } = &l.kind
            {
                if let (Some(a), Some(c)) = (as_int(a), ri) {
                    if ast_fold_mul(a, c).is_some() {
                        *changed = true;
                        return bin(
                            Add,
                            bin(Mul, (**x).clone(), int(c), sp),
                            int(a.wrapping_mul(c)),
                            sp,
                        );
                    }
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Sub,
                l: x,
                r: a,
            } = &l.kind
            {
                if let (Some(a), Some(c)) = (as_int(a), ri) {
                    if ast_fold_mul(a, c).is_some() {
                        *changed = true;
                        return bin(
                            Sub,
                            bin(Mul, (**x).clone(), int(c), sp),
                            int(a.wrapping_mul(c)),
                            sp,
                        );
                    }
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Add,
                l: x,
                r: a,
            } = &r.kind
            {
                if let (Some(c), Some(a)) = (li, as_int(a)) {
                    if ast_fold_mul(c, a).is_some() {
                        *changed = true;
                        return bin(
                            Add,
                            bin(Mul, int(c), (**x).clone(), sp),
                            int(c.wrapping_mul(a)),
                            sp,
                        );
                    }
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Sub,
                l: x,
                r: a,
            } = &r.kind
            {
                if let (Some(c), Some(a)) = (li, as_int(a)) {
                    if ast_fold_mul(c, a).is_some() {
                        *changed = true;
                        return bin(
                            Sub,
                            bin(Mul, int(c), (**x).clone(), sp),
                            int(c.wrapping_mul(a)),
                            sp,
                        );
                    }
                }
            }
            if li.is_some() && ri.is_none() && is_pure(&l, pure) && is_pure(&r, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return bin(Mul, r, l, sp);
            }
        }
        Div => {
            // Do not rewrite signed integer division by powers of two into a
            // right shift: division truncates toward zero, while signed right
            // shift typically rounds negative values toward minus infinity.
            if ri == Some(1) {
                *changed = true;
                return l;
            }
            if li == Some(0) && ri.is_some() && ri != Some(0) && is_pure(&r, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return int(0);
            }
        }
        Rem => {
            // `% power_of_two` is not generally equivalent to `& (n - 1)`
            // for negative signed operands. Keep the typed operation intact.
            if ri == Some(1) && is_pure(&l, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return int(0);
            }
            if li == Some(0) && ri.is_some() && ri != Some(0) && is_pure(&r, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return int(0);
            }
        }
        And => {
            if ri == Some(0) && is_pure(&l, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return int(0);
            }
            if li == Some(0) && is_pure(&r, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return int(0);
            }
            if ri == Some(u64::MAX) {
                *changed = true;
                return l;
            }
            if li == Some(u64::MAX) {
                *changed = true;
                return r;
            }
            if same(&l, &r) && is_pure(&l, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return l;
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: And,
                l: x,
                r: a,
            } = &l.kind
            {
                if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (as_int(a), ri) {
                    if let Some(value) = ast_fold_bitwise(And, a, b) {
                        *changed = true;
                        return bin(And, (**x).clone(), int(value), sp);
                    }
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin { op: Or, l: x, r: y } = &r.kind {
                if same(&l, x) && is_pure(&l, pure) && is_pure(y, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return l;
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: And,
                l: x,
                r: _y,
            } = &r.kind
            {
                if same(&l, x) && is_pure(&l, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return r;
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin { op: Or, l: x, r: y } = &l.kind {
                if same(x, &r) && is_pure(x, pure) && is_pure(y, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return r;
                }
            }
            if let (
                ExprKind::Bin {
                    op: And,
                    l: x1,
                    r: a,
                },
                ExprKind::Bin {
                    op: And,
                    l: x2,
                    r: b,
                },
            ) = (&l.kind, &r.kind)
            {
                if same(x1, x2) && is_pure(x1, pure) {
                    if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (as_int(a), as_int(b)) {
                        if let Some(value) = ast_fold_bitwise(And, a, b) {
                            *changed = true;
                            return bin(And, (**x1).clone(), int(value), sp);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            if let (
                ExprKind::Bin {
                    op: Or,
                    l: x1,
                    r: a,
                },
                ExprKind::Bin {
                    op: Or,
                    l: x2,
                    r: b,
                },
            ) = (&l.kind, &r.kind)
            {
                if same(x1, x2) && is_pure(x1, pure) {
                    if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (as_int(a), as_int(b)) {
                        if let Some(value) = ast_fold_bitwise(And, a, b) {
                            *changed = true;
                            return bin(Or, (**x1).clone(), int(value), sp);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: And,
                l: _y,
                r: x,
            } = &r.kind
            {
                if same(&l, x) && is_pure(&l, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return r;
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Xor,
                l: x,
                r: y,
            } = &r.kind
            {
                if same(&l, x) && is_pure(&l, pure) && is_pure(y, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return bin(
                        And,
                        l,
                        Expr {
                            span: sp,
                            kind: ExprKind::Un {
                                op: UnOp::BitNot,
                                e: y.clone(),
                            },
                        },
                        sp,
                    );
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Xor,
                l: x,
                r: y,
            } = &l.kind
            {
                if same(x, &r) && is_pure(x, pure) && is_pure(y, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return bin(
                        And,
                        r,
                        Expr {
                            span: sp,
                            kind: ExprKind::Un {
                                op: UnOp::BitNot,
                                e: y.clone(),
                            },
                        },
                        sp,
                    );
                }
            }
        }
        Or => {
            if ri == Some(0) {
                *changed = true;
                return l;
            }
            if li == Some(0) {
                *changed = true;
                return r;
            }
            if ri == Some(u64::MAX) && is_pure(&l, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return int(u64::MAX);
            }
            if li == Some(u64::MAX) && is_pure(&r, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return int(u64::MAX);
            }
            if same(&l, &r) && is_pure(&l, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return l;
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin { op: Or, l: x, r: a } = &l.kind {
                if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (as_int(a), ri) {
                    if let Some(value) = ast_fold_bitwise(Or, a, b) {
                        *changed = true;
                        return bin(Or, (**x).clone(), int(value), sp);
                    }
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: And,
                l: x,
                r: y,
            } = &r.kind
            {
                if same(&l, x) && is_pure(&l, pure) && is_pure(y, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return l;
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Or,
                l: x,
                r: _y,
            } = &r.kind
            {
                if same(&l, x) && is_pure(&l, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return r;
                }
            }
            if let (
                ExprKind::Bin {
                    op: And,
                    l: x1,
                    r: a,
                },
                ExprKind::Bin {
                    op: And,
                    l: x2,
                    r: b,
                },
            ) = (&l.kind, &r.kind)
            {
                if same(x1, x2) && is_pure(x1, pure) {
                    if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (as_int(a), as_int(b)) {
                        if let Some(value) = ast_fold_bitwise(Or, a, b) {
                            *changed = true;
                            return bin(And, (**x1).clone(), int(value), sp);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            if let (
                ExprKind::Bin {
                    op: And,
                    l: x1,
                    r: a,
                },
                ExprKind::Bin {
                    op: Or,
                    l: x2,
                    r: _b,
                },
            ) = (&l.kind, &r.kind)
            {
                if same(x1, x2) && is_pure(x1, pure) && is_pure(a, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return r.clone();
                }
            }
            if let (
                ExprKind::Bin {
                    op: Or,
                    l: x1,
                    r: a,
                },
                ExprKind::Bin {
                    op: Or,
                    l: x2,
                    r: b,
                },
            ) = (&l.kind, &r.kind)
            {
                if same(x1, x2) && is_pure(x1, pure) {
                    if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (as_int(a), as_int(b)) {
                        if let Some(value) = ast_fold_bitwise(Or, a, b) {
                            *changed = true;
                            return bin(Or, (**x1).clone(), int(value), sp);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Xor,
                l: x,
                r: y,
            } = &r.kind
            {
                if same(&l, x) && is_pure(&l, pure) && is_pure(y, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return bin(Or, l, (**y).clone(), sp);
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Xor,
                l: x,
                r: y,
            } = &l.kind
            {
                if same(x, &r) && is_pure(x, pure) && is_pure(y, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return bin(Or, r, (**y).clone(), sp);
                }
            }
        }
        Xor => {
            if ri == Some(0) {
                *changed = true;
                return l;
            }
            if li == Some(0) {
                *changed = true;
                return r;
            }
            if ri == Some(u64::MAX) {
                *changed = true;
                return Expr {
                    span: sp,
                    kind: ExprKind::Un {
                        op: UnOp::BitNot,
                        e: Box::new(l),
                    },
                };
            }
            if li == Some(u64::MAX) {
                *changed = true;
                return Expr {
                    span: sp,
                    kind: ExprKind::Un {
                        op: UnOp::BitNot,
                        e: Box::new(r),
                    },
                };
            }
            if same(&l, &r) && is_pure(&l, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return int(0);
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Xor,
                l: x,
                r: a,
            } = &l.kind
            {
                if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (as_int(a), ri) {
                    if let Some(value) = ast_fold_bitwise(Xor, a, b) {
                        *changed = true;
                        return bin(Xor, (**x).clone(), int(value), sp);
                    }
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Xor,
                l: x,
                r: y,
            } = &r.kind
            {
                if same(&l, x) && is_pure(&l, pure) && is_pure(y, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return (**y).clone();
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Xor,
                l: x,
                r: y,
            } = &l.kind
            {
                if same(y, &r) && is_pure(&r, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return (**x).clone();
                }
            }
            if let (
                ExprKind::Bin {
                    op: Xor,
                    l: x1,
                    r: a,
                },
                ExprKind::Bin {
                    op: Xor,
                    l: x2,
                    r: b,
                },
            ) = (&l.kind, &r.kind)
            {
                if same(x1, x2) && is_pure(x1, pure) {
                    if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (as_int(a), as_int(b)) {
                        if let Some(value) = ast_fold_bitwise(Xor, a, b) {
                            *changed = true;
                            return int(value);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin { op: Or, l: x, r: y } = &r.kind {
                if same(&l, x) && is_pure(&l, pure) && is_pure(y, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return bin(
                        And,
                        Expr {
                            span: sp,
                            kind: ExprKind::Un {
                                op: UnOp::BitNot,
                                e: x.clone(),
                            },
                        },
                        (**y).clone(),
                        sp,
                    );
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: And,
                l: x,
                r: y,
            } = &r.kind
            {
                if same(&l, x) && is_pure(&l, pure) && is_pure(y, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return bin(
                        And,
                        l,
                        Expr {
                            span: sp,
                            kind: ExprKind::Un {
                                op: UnOp::BitNot,
                                e: y.clone(),
                            },
                        },
                        sp,
                    );
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: And,
                l: x,
                r: y,
            } = &l.kind
            {
                if same(x, &r) && is_pure(x, pure) && is_pure(y, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return bin(
                        And,
                        r,
                        Expr {
                            span: sp,
                            kind: ExprKind::Un {
                                op: UnOp::BitNot,
                                e: y.clone(),
                            },
                        },
                        sp,
                    );
                }
            }
        }
        Shl | Shr => {
            if ri == Some(0) {
                *changed = true;
                return l;
            }
            if let Some(b) = ri {
                let masked = b & 63;
                if masked != b {
                    *changed = true;
                    return bin(op, l, int(masked), sp);
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: iop,
                l: x,
                r: a,
            } = &l.kind
            {
                if *iop == op {
                    if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (as_int(a), ri) {
                        *changed = true;
                        return bin(op, (**x).clone(), int(a.wrapping_add(b) & 63), sp);
                    }
                }
            }
            if li == Some(0) && is_pure(&r, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return int(0);
            }
        }
        Eq | Ne => {
            if li.is_some() && ri.is_none() && is_pure(&l, pure) && is_pure(&r, pure) {
                let out = op;
                *changed = true;
                return bin(out, r, l, sp);
            }
            if same(&l, &r) && is_pure(&l, pure) && is_int_expr(&l) {
                *changed = true;
                return int(if op == Eq { 1 } else { 0 });
            }
            if is_bool(&l) && ri == Some(1) {
                *changed = true;
                return l;
            }
            if is_bool(&l) && op == Ne && ri == Some(0) {
                *changed = true;
                return l;
            }
            if is_bool(&l) && op == Eq && ri == Some(0) {
                *changed = true;
                return Expr {
                    span: sp,
                    kind: ExprKind::Un {
                        op: UnOp::Not,
                        e: Box::new(l),
                    },
                };
            }
            if is_bool(&l) && op == Ne && ri == Some(1) {
                *changed = true;
                return Expr {
                    span: sp,
                    kind: ExprKind::Un {
                        op: UnOp::Not,
                        e: Box::new(l),
                    },
                };
            }
        }
        Lt | Gt | Le | Ge => {
            if li.is_some() && ri.is_none() && is_pure(&l, pure) && is_pure(&r, pure) {
                let rev = match op {
                    Lt => Gt,
                    Gt => Lt,
                    Le => Ge,
                    Ge => Le,
                    _ => op,
                };
                *changed = true;
                return bin(rev, r, l, sp);
            }
        }
        LAnd => {
            if ri == Some(1) {
                *changed = true;
                return bin(Ne, l, int(0), sp);
            }
            if li == Some(1) {
                *changed = true;
                return bin(Ne, r, int(0), sp);
            }
            if ri == Some(0) && is_pure(&l, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return int(0);
            }
            if li == Some(0) && is_pure(&r, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return int(0);
            }
            if same(&l, &r) && is_pure(&l, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return bin(Ne, l, int(0), sp);
            }
        }
        LOr => {
            if ri == Some(0) {
                *changed = true;
                return bin(Ne, l, int(0), sp);
            }
            if li == Some(0) {
                *changed = true;
                return bin(Ne, r, int(0), sp);
            }
            if ri == Some(1) && is_pure(&l, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return int(1);
            }
            if li == Some(1) && is_pure(&r, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return int(1);
            }
            if same(&l, &r) && is_pure(&l, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return bin(Ne, l, int(0), sp);
            }
        }
    }

    Expr {
        span: sp,
        kind: ExprKind::Bin {
            op,
            l: Box::new(l),
            r: Box::new(r),
        },
    }
}

fn simp_let(e: Expr, pure: &HashSet<String>, changed: &mut bool) -> Expr {
    let sp = e.span;
    let ExprKind::Let { name, value, body } = e.kind else {
        return e;
    };
    let value = *value;
    let body = *body;
    let uses = count_var(&body, &name);

    if matches!(&body.kind, ExprKind::Var(n) if n == &name) {
        *changed = true;
        return value;
    }
    if uses == 0 && is_pure(&value, pure) {
        *changed = true;
        return body;
    }
    // Substituting an effectful initializer into the body can reorder its
    // effects relative to other body expressions. Only inline a one-use value
    // when it is referentially transparent. Re-materialize cheap literals and
    // variable references for multiple uses, but keep expensive pure work in
    // one let so it is not recomputed several times.
    if uses == 1 && is_pure(&value, pure) {
        let b = subst(body.clone(), &name, &value);
        if count_var(&b, &name) == 0 {
            *changed = true;
            return b;
        }
    }
    let cheap_to_rematerialize = matches!(
        &value.kind,
        ExprKind::Var(_) | ExprKind::Int(_) | ExprKind::Float(_) | ExprKind::Str(_)
    );
    if uses > 1 && is_pure(&value, pure) && cheap_to_rematerialize {
        let b = subst(body.clone(), &name, &value);
        if count_var(&b, &name) == 0 {
            *changed = true;
            return b;
        }
    }
    if let ExprKind::Let {
        name: inner,
        value: iv,
        body: ib,
    } = &value.kind
    {
        if count_var(iv, &name) == 0 {
            *changed = true;
            let rebound = Expr {
                span: sp,
                kind: ExprKind::Let {
                    name: name.clone(),
                    value: ib.clone(),
                    body: Box::new(body),
                },
            };
            return Expr {
                span: sp,
                kind: ExprKind::Let {
                    name: inner.clone(),
                    value: iv.clone(),
                    body: Box::new(rebound),
                },
            };
        }
    }
    if let ExprKind::Let {
        name: inner,
        value: iv,
        body: ib,
    } = &body.kind
    {
        if count_var(iv, &name) == 0 && is_pure(&value, pure) {
            let nb = subst((**ib).clone(), &name, &value);
            if count_var(&nb, &name) == 0 {
                *changed = true;
                return Expr {
                    span: sp,
                    kind: ExprKind::Let {
                        name: inner.clone(),
                        value: iv.clone(),
                        body: Box::new(nb),
                    },
                };
            }
        }
    }
    if matches!(
        &value.kind,
        ExprKind::Var(_) | ExprKind::Int(_) | ExprKind::Float(_)
    ) && uses > 0 && is_pure(&value, pure)
    {
        let b = subst(body.clone(), &name, &value);
        if count_var(&b, &name) == 0 {
            *changed = true;
            return b;
        }
    }
    Expr {
        span: sp,
        kind: ExprKind::Let {
            name,
            value: Box::new(value),
            body: Box::new(body),
        },
    }
}

fn simp_app(e: Expr, pure: &HashSet<String>, changed: &mut bool) -> Expr {
    let sp = e.span;
    let ExprKind::App { fun, args } = e.kind else {
        return e;
    };
    let fun = *fun;

    if let ExprKind::Fn { params, body } = &fun.kind {
        if params.len() == args.len() && distinct(params) {
            let mut b = (**body).clone();
            let mut ok = true;
            for (param, arg) in params.iter().zip(args.iter()) {
                let n = count_var(&b, param);
                if n == 0 {
                    if !is_pure(arg, pure) {
                        ok = false;
                        break;
                    }
                } else if n == 1 || is_pure(arg, pure) {
                    b = subst(b, param, arg);
                } else {
                    ok = false;
                    break;
                }
                if count_var(&b, param) != 0 {
                    ok = false;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if ok {
                *changed = true;
                return b;
            }
        }
    }

    if let ExprKind::Fn { params, body } = &fun.kind {
        if params.len() == 1
            && args.len() == 1
            && matches!(&body.kind, ExprKind::Var(n) if n == &params[0])
        {
            *changed = true;
            return args.into_iter().next().unwrap();
        }
    }

    if let ExprKind::Fn { params, body } = &fun.kind {
        if params.len() == args.len()
            && params.iter().enumerate().all(
                |(i, p)| matches!(args.get(i).map(|x| &x.kind), Some(ExprKind::Var(v)) if v == p),
            )
        {
            if let ExprKind::App { fun: target, .. } = &body.kind {
                if params.iter().all(|p| count_var(target, p) == 0) && is_pure(target, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return (**target).clone();
                }
            }
        }
    }

    Expr {
        span: sp,
        kind: ExprKind::App {
            fun: Box::new(fun),
            args,
        },
    }
}

fn simp_case(e: Expr, pure: &HashSet<String>, changed: &mut bool) -> Expr {
    let sp = e.span;
    let ExprKind::Case { scrut, arms } = e.kind else {
        return e;
    };
    let scrut = *scrut;
    let mut arms = arms;

    if let Some(first) = arms.first() {
        if matches!(first.pat, Pat::Wild) {
            if is_pure(&scrut, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return first.body.clone();
            }
            *changed = true;
            return Expr {
                span: sp,
                kind: ExprKind::Block {
                    stmts: vec![scrut],
                    tail: Box::new(first.body.clone()),
                },
            };
        }
        if let Pat::Bind(name) = &first.pat {
            let uses = count_var(&first.body, name);
            if uses == 1 || is_pure(&scrut, pure) {
                let b = subst(first.body.clone(), name, &scrut);
                if count_var(&b, name) == 0 {
                    *changed = true;
                    return b;
                }
            }
            if uses == 0 && !is_pure(&scrut, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return Expr {
                    span: sp,
                    kind: ExprKind::Block {
                        stmts: vec![scrut],
                        tail: Box::new(first.body.clone()),
                    },
                };
            }
        }
    }

    let mut seen = Vec::<Pat>::new();
    let mut kept = Vec::<Arm>::with_capacity(arms.len());
    for arm in arms {
        if seen.iter().any(|p| same_pat(p, &arm.pat)) {
            *changed = true;
            continue;
        }
        let stop = matches!(arm.pat, Pat::Wild | Pat::Bind(_));
        seen.push(arm.pat.clone());
        kept.push(arm);
        if stop {
            *changed = true;
            break;
        }
    }
    arms = kept;

    if let Some(v) = as_int(&scrut) {
        for arm in &arms {
            match &arm.pat {
                Pat::Int(pv) if *pv == v => {
                    *changed = true;
                    return arm.body.clone();
                }
                Pat::Bind(name) => {
                    let uses = count_var(&arm.body, name);
                    if uses <= 1 || is_pure(&scrut, pure) {
                        let b = subst(arm.body.clone(), name, &scrut);
                        if count_var(&b, name) == 0 {
                            *changed = true;
                            return b;
                        }
                    }
                }
                Pat::Wild => {
                    *changed = true;
                    return arm.body.clone();
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }
    }

    if let ExprKind::Str(bytes) = &scrut.kind {
        for arm in &arms {
            match &arm.pat {
                Pat::Str(pb) if pb == bytes => {
                    *changed = true;
                    return arm.body.clone();
                }
                Pat::Bind(name) => {
                    let uses = count_var(&arm.body, name);
                    if uses <= 1 || is_pure(&scrut, pure) {
                        let b = subst(arm.body.clone(), name, &scrut);
                        if count_var(&b, name) == 0 {
                            *changed = true;
                            return b;
                        }
                    }
                }
                Pat::Wild => {
                    *changed = true;
                    return arm.body.clone();
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }
    }

    if arms.len() == 1 && is_pure(&scrut, pure) {
        if let Pat::Bind(name) = &arms[0].pat {
            let b = subst(arms[0].body.clone(), name, &scrut);
            if count_var(&b, name) == 0 {
                *changed = true;
                return b;
            }
        }
        if matches!(arms[0].pat, Pat::Wild) {
            *changed = true;
            return arms[0].body.clone();
        }
    }

    if arms.len() > 1 {
        let first = &arms[0].body;
        if arms.iter().all(|a| same(first, &a.body)) {
            if arms
                .iter()
                .all(|a| matches!(a.pat, Pat::Wild | Pat::Bind(_)))
            {
                *changed = true;
                return first.clone();
            }
        }
    }

    Expr {
        span: sp,
        kind: ExprKind::Case {
            scrut: Box::new(scrut),
            arms,
        },
    }
}

fn simp_block(e: Expr, pure: &HashSet<String>, changed: &mut bool) -> Expr {
    let sp = e.span;
    let ExprKind::Block { stmts, tail } = e.kind else {
        return e;
    };
    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(stmts.len());

    for s in stmts {
        match s.kind {
            ExprKind::Block {
                stmts: inner,
                tail: it,
            } => {
                *changed = true;
                out.extend(inner);
                out.push(*it);
            }
            _ => out.push(s),
        }
    }

    let tail_span = tail.span;
    let tail = match tail.kind {
        ExprKind::Block {
            stmts: inner,
            tail: inner_tail,
        } => {
            *changed = true;
            out.extend(inner);
            *inner_tail
        }
        kind => Expr {
            span: tail_span,
            kind,
        },
    };

    let before = out.len();
    out.retain(|s| !is_pure(s, pure));
    if before != out.len() {
        *changed = true;
    }

    if out.is_empty() {
        *changed = true;
        return tail;
    }

    let mut dedup = Vec::with_capacity(out.len());
    for s in out {
        if is_pure(&s, pure) && dedup.iter().any(|x| same(x, &s)) {
            *changed = true;
        } else {
            dedup.push(s);
        }
    }

    if dedup.len() == 1 && same(&dedup[0], &tail) && is_pure(&dedup[0], pure) {
        *changed = true;
        return tail;
    }

    Expr {
        span: sp,
        kind: ExprKind::Block {
            stmts: dedup,
            tail: Box::new(tail),
        },
    }
}

fn adv_opt(mut p: Program, pure: &HashSet<String>, changed: &mut bool) -> Program {
    let mut used = identifiers(&p);
    let mut serial = 0usize;
    p = map_prog(p, &mut |e| {
        adv_expr(e, pure, changed, &mut used, &mut serial)
    });
    adv_case(p, pure, changed, &mut used, &mut serial)
}

fn identifiers(p: &Program) -> HashSet<String> {
    let mut out = HashSet::new();
    fn pat(p: &Pat, out: &mut HashSet<String>) {
        match p {
            Pat::Bind(n) => {
                out.insert(n.clone());
            }
            Pat::Ctor { name, args } => {
                out.insert(name.clone());
                for a in args {
                    pat(a, out);
                }
            }
            Pat::Tuple(xs) => {
                for x in xs {
                    pat(x, out);
                }
            }
            Pat::Wild | Pat::Int(_) | Pat::Str(_) => {}
        }
    }
    fn expr(e: &Expr, out: &mut HashSet<String>) {
        match &e.kind {
            ExprKind::Var(n) => {
                out.insert(n.clone());
            }
            ExprKind::Let { name, value, body } => {
                out.insert(name.clone());
                expr(value, out);
                expr(body, out);
            }
            ExprKind::Fn { params, body } => {
                for n in params {
                    out.insert(n.clone());
                }
                expr(body, out);
            }
            ExprKind::App { fun, args } => {
                expr(fun, out);
                for a in args {
                    expr(a, out);
                }
            }
            ExprKind::Case { scrut, arms } => {
                expr(scrut, out);
                for a in arms {
                    pat(&a.pat, out);
                    expr(&a.body, out);
                }
            }
            ExprKind::Block { stmts, tail } => {
                for x in stmts {
                    expr(x, out);
                }
                expr(tail, out);
            }
            ExprKind::Bin { l, r, .. } => {
                expr(l, out);
                expr(r, out);
            }
            ExprKind::Un { e, .. } | ExprKind::Quote(e) => expr(e, out),
            ExprKind::Int(_) | ExprKind::Float(_) | ExprKind::Str(_) => {}
        }
    }
    fn ty(t: &Ty, out: &mut HashSet<String>) {
        match t {
            Ty::Named(n) => {
                out.insert(n.clone());
            }
            Ty::App(n, xs) => {
                out.insert(n.clone());
                for x in xs {
                    ty(x, out);
                }
            }
            Ty::Array(x) => ty(x, out),
            Ty::Tuple(xs) => {
                for x in xs {
                    ty(x, out);
                }
            }
            Ty::Fun(a, b) => {
                ty(a, out);
                ty(b, out);
            }
            Ty::Prim(_) | Ty::Bottom => {}
        }
    }
    for d in &p.decls {
        match d {
            Decl::Let { name, value, .. } => {
                out.insert(name.clone());
                expr(value, &mut out);
            }
            Decl::External(e) => {
                out.insert(e.name.clone());
            }
            Decl::Type { name, ctors, .. } => {
                out.insert(name.clone());
                for c in ctors {
                    out.insert(c.name.clone());
                    for t in &c.args {
                        ty(t, &mut out);
                    }
                }
            }
            Decl::Bring(_) => {}
        }
    }
    expr(&p.body, &mut out);
    out
}

fn fresh_opt_name(used: &mut HashSet<String>, serial: &mut usize, stem: &str) -> String {
    loop {
        let n = format!("__mak_opt_{}_{}", stem, *serial);
        *serial += 1;
        if used.insert(n.clone()) {
            return n;
        }
    }
}

fn assoc(op: BinOp) -> bool {
    matches!(
        op,
        BinOp::Add | BinOp::Mul | BinOp::And | BinOp::Or | BinOp::Xor
    )
}

fn flatten_assoc(op: BinOp, e: &Expr, out: &mut Vec<Expr>) {
    if let ExprKind::Bin { op: inner, l, r } = &e.kind {
        if *inner == op {
            flatten_assoc(op, l, out);
            flatten_assoc(op, r, out);
            return;
        }
    }
    out.push(e.clone());
}

fn rebuild_assoc(op: BinOp, xs: &[Expr], sp: Span) -> Expr {
    let mut it = xs.iter();
    let mut acc = it.next().cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| int(0));
    for x in it {
        acc = bin(op, acc, x.clone(), sp);
    }
    acc
}

fn canon_assoc(e: Expr, pure: &HashSet<String>, changed: &mut bool) -> Expr {
    let sp = e.span;
    let ExprKind::Bin { op, .. } = &e.kind else {
        return e;
    };
    if !assoc(*op) {
        return e;
    }
    let mut xs = Vec::new();
    flatten_assoc(*op, &e, &mut xs);
    if xs.len() < 3 || !xs.iter().all(|x| is_pure(x, pure)) {
        return e;
    }
    let mut constants = Vec::new();
    let mut nonconst = Vec::new();
    for x in xs {
        if let Some(v) = as_int(&x) {
            constants.push(v);
        } else {
            nonconst.push(x);
        }
    }
    if constants.len() < 2 {
        return e;
    }
    // Keep AST-level reassociation within the representation the AST can
    // express. Typed LIR can handle U64 constants after type inference.
    if constants.iter().any(|v| *v > AST_INT_FOLD_MAX) {
        return e;
    }
    let folded = match op {
        BinOp::Add => match constants.into_iter().try_fold(0u64, ast_fold_add) {
            Some(v) => v,
            None => return e,
        },
        BinOp::Mul => match constants.into_iter().try_fold(1u64, ast_fold_mul) {
            Some(v) => v,
            None => return e,
        },
        BinOp::And => constants.into_iter().fold(u64::MAX, |a, b| a & b),
        BinOp::Or => constants.into_iter().fold(0u64, |a, b| a | b),
        BinOp::Xor => constants.into_iter().fold(0u64, |a, b| a ^ b),
        _ => return e,
    };
    let neutral = match op {
        BinOp::Add | BinOp::Or | BinOp::Xor => folded == 0,
        BinOp::Mul => folded == 1,
        BinOp::And => folded == u64::MAX,
        _ => false,
    };
    if neutral && nonconst.is_empty() {
        *changed = true;
        return int(folded);
    }
    if neutral && !nonconst.is_empty() {
        *changed = true;
        return rebuild_assoc(*op, &nonconst, sp);
    }
    nonconst.push(int(folded));
    *changed = true;
    rebuild_assoc(*op, &nonconst, sp)
}

fn invert(op: BinOp) -> Option<BinOp> {
    Some(match op {
        BinOp::Eq => BinOp::Ne,
        BinOp::Ne => BinOp::Eq,
        BinOp::Lt => BinOp::Ge,
        BinOp::Le => BinOp::Gt,
        BinOp::Gt => BinOp::Le,
        BinOp::Ge => BinOp::Lt,
        _ => return None,
    })
}

fn adv_un(e: Expr, pure: &HashSet<String>, changed: &mut bool) -> Expr {
    let sp = e.span;
    let ExprKind::Un {
        op: UnOp::Not,
        e: inner,
    } = &e.kind
    else {
        return e;
    };
    if let ExprKind::Bin { op, l, r } = &inner.kind {
        if matches!(op, BinOp::LAnd | BinOp::LOr) {
            *changed = true;
            let bop = if *op == BinOp::LAnd {
                BinOp::LOr
            } else {
                BinOp::LAnd
            };
            return bin(
                bop,
                Expr {
                    span: sp,
                    kind: ExprKind::Un {
                        op: UnOp::Not,
                        e: l.clone(),
                    },
                },
                Expr {
                    span: sp,
                    kind: ExprKind::Un {
                        op: UnOp::Not,
                        e: r.clone(),
                    },
                },
                sp,
            );
        }
        if let Some(inv) = invert(*op) {
            *changed = true;
            return bin(inv, (**l).clone(), (**r).clone(), sp);
        }
    }
    let _ = pure;
    e
}

fn pat_shape_eq(a: &Pat, b: &Pat) -> bool {
    match (a, b) {
        (Pat::Wild, Pat::Wild) => true,
        (Pat::Int(x), Pat::Int(y)) => x == y,
        (Pat::Str(x), Pat::Str(y)) => x == y,
        (Pat::Bind(_), Pat::Bind(_)) => true,
        (Pat::Ctor { name: an, args: aa }, Pat::Ctor { name: bn, args: ba }) => {
            an == bn && aa.len() == ba.len() && aa.iter().zip(ba).all(|(x, y)| pat_shape_eq(x, y))
        }
        (Pat::Tuple(ax), Pat::Tuple(bx)) => {
            ax.len() == bx.len() && ax.iter().zip(bx).all(|(x, y)| pat_shape_eq(x, y))
        }
        _ => false,
    }
}

fn arm_uses(arm: &Arm) -> bool {
    let mut names = Vec::new();
    pat_binders(&arm.pat, &mut names);
    names.iter().any(|n| count_var(&arm.body, n) != 0)
}

fn preserve_scrut(
    scrut: Expr,
    body: Expr,
    pure: &HashSet<String>,
    used: &mut HashSet<String>,
    serial: &mut usize,
) -> Expr {
    if is_pure(&scrut, pure) {
        body
    } else {
        let name = fresh_opt_name(used, serial, "scrut");
        Expr {
            span: body.span,
            kind: ExprKind::Let {
                name,
                value: Box::new(scrut),
                body: Box::new(body),
            },
        }
    }
}

fn prune_arms(
    scrut: Expr,
    mut arms: Vec<Arm>,
    pure: &HashSet<String>,
    changed: &mut bool,
    used: &mut HashSet<String>,
    serial: &mut usize,
) -> Expr {
    let mut seen = Vec::<Pat>::new();
    let mut kept = Vec::with_capacity(arms.len());
    for arm in arms.drain(..) {
        if seen.iter().any(|p| pat_shape_eq(p, &arm.pat)) {
            *changed = true;
            continue;
        }
        let total = matches!(arm.pat, Pat::Wild | Pat::Bind(_));
        seen.push(arm.pat.clone());
        kept.push(arm);
        if total {
            if kept.len() != seen.len() {
                *changed = true;
            }
            break;
        }
    }
    if kept.len() != seen.len() {
        *changed = true;
    }
    if kept.is_empty() {
        return Expr {
            span: scrut.span,
            kind: ExprKind::Case {
                scrut: Box::new(scrut),
                arms: Vec::new(),
            },
        };
    }
    if kept.len() == 1 && matches!(kept[0].pat, Pat::Wild | Pat::Bind(_)) {
        let arm = kept.remove(0);
        *changed = true;

        match arm.pat {
            Pat::Wild => {
                return preserve_scrut(scrut, arm.body, pure, used, serial);
            }
            Pat::Bind(n) => {
                if is_pure(&scrut, pure) {
                    return subst(arm.body, &n, &scrut);
                }

                let bind_name = fresh_opt_name(used, serial, "scrut");
                let bound = Expr {
                    span: arm.body.span,
                    kind: ExprKind::Var(bind_name.clone()),
                };
                let body = subst(arm.body, &n, &bound);
                return Expr {
                    span: body.span,
                    kind: ExprKind::Let {
                        name: bind_name,
                        value: Box::new(scrut),
                        body: Box::new(body),
                    },
                };
            }
            _ => unreachable!(),
        }
    }
    if kept.len() >= 2 {
        let same_body = kept.windows(2).all(|w| same(&w[0].body, &w[1].body));
        if same_body && exhaustive(&kept) && kept.iter().all(|a| !arm_uses(a)) {
            *changed = true;
            return preserve_scrut(scrut, kept[0].body.clone(), pure, used, serial);
        }
    }
    if kept.len() != seen.len() {
        *changed = true;
    }
    Expr {
        span: scrut.span,
        kind: ExprKind::Case {
            scrut: Box::new(scrut),
            arms: kept,
        },
    }
}

fn adv_case(
    p: Program,
    pure: &HashSet<String>,
    changed: &mut bool,
    used: &mut HashSet<String>,
    serial: &mut usize,
) -> Program {
    fn go(
        e: Expr,
        pure: &HashSet<String>,
        changed: &mut bool,
        used: &mut HashSet<String>,
        serial: &mut usize,
    ) -> Expr {
        let e = map_child(e, &mut |x| go(x, pure, changed, used, serial));
        if let ExprKind::Case { scrut, arms } = e.kind {
            return prune_arms(*scrut, arms, pure, changed, used, serial);
        }
        e
    }
    map_prog(p, &mut |e| go(e, pure, changed, used, serial))
}

fn binder_set(p: &Pat, out: &mut HashSet<String>) {
    match p {
        Pat::Bind(n) => {
            out.insert(n.clone());
        }
        Pat::Ctor { args, .. } => {
            for a in args {
                binder_set(a, out);
            }
        }
        Pat::Tuple(xs) => {
            for x in xs {
                binder_set(x, out);
            }
        }
        Pat::Wild | Pat::Int(_) | Pat::Str(_) => {}
    }
}

fn uses_any(e: &Expr, names: &HashSet<String>) -> bool {
    let mut found = false;
    walk(e, &mut |x| {
        if let ExprKind::Var(n) = &x.kind {
            if names.contains(n) {
                found = true;
            }
        }
    });
    found
}

fn arm_caps(arm: &Arm, e: &Expr) -> bool {
    let mut binders = HashSet::new();
    binder_set(&arm.pat, &mut binders);
    uses_any(e, &binders)
}

fn exhaustive(arms: &[Arm]) -> bool {
    arms.iter()
        .any(|a| matches!(a.pat, Pat::Wild | Pat::Bind(_)))
}

fn distribute(e: Expr, pure: &HashSet<String>, changed: &mut bool) -> Expr {
    let sp = e.span;
    match e.kind {
        ExprKind::App { fun, args } => {
            for i in 0..args.len() {
                if let ExprKind::Case { scrut, arms } = &args[i].kind {
                    if !is_pure(&fun, pure) || args[..i].iter().any(|a| !is_pure(a, pure)) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if arms.iter().any(|arm| {
                        arm_caps(arm, &fun)
                            || args
                                .iter()
                                .enumerate()
                                .any(|(j, a)| j != i && arm_caps(arm, a))
                    }) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(arms.len());
                    for arm in arms {
                        let mut na = args.clone();
                        na[i] = arm.body.clone();
                        out.push(Arm {
                            pat: arm.pat.clone(),
                            span: arm.span,
                            body: Expr {
                                span: sp,
                                kind: ExprKind::App {
                                    fun: fun.clone(),
                                    args: na,
                                },
                            },
                        });
                    }
                    *changed = true;
                    return Expr {
                        span: sp,
                        kind: ExprKind::Case {
                            scrut: scrut.clone(),
                            arms: out,
                        },
                    };
                }
            }
            Expr {
                span: sp,
                kind: ExprKind::App { fun, args },
            }
        }
        ExprKind::Un { op, e: inner } => {
            if let ExprKind::Case { scrut, arms } = &inner.kind {
                *changed = true;
                let out = arms
                    .iter()
                    .map(|a| Arm {
                        pat: a.pat.clone(),
                        span: a.span,
                        body: Expr {
                            span: sp,
                            kind: ExprKind::Un {
                                op,
                                e: Box::new(a.body.clone()),
                            },
                        },
                    })
                    .collect();
                Expr {
                    span: sp,
                    kind: ExprKind::Case {
                        scrut: scrut.clone(),
                        arms: out,
                    },
                }
            } else {
                Expr {
                    span: sp,
                    kind: ExprKind::Un { op, e: inner },
                }
            }
        }
        ExprKind::Bin { op, l, r } => {
            if let ExprKind::Case { scrut, arms } = &l.kind {
                if arms.iter().any(|arm| arm_caps(arm, &r)) {
                    return Expr {
                        span: sp,
                        kind: ExprKind::Bin { op, l, r },
                    };
                }
                let out = arms
                    .iter()
                    .map(|a| Arm {
                        pat: a.pat.clone(),
                        span: a.span,
                        body: Expr {
                            span: sp,
                            kind: ExprKind::Bin {
                                op,
                                l: Box::new(a.body.clone()),
                                r: r.clone(),
                            },
                        },
                    })
                    .collect();
                *changed = true;
                return Expr {
                    span: sp,
                    kind: ExprKind::Case {
                        scrut: scrut.clone(),
                        arms: out,
                    },
                };
            }
            if let ExprKind::Case { scrut, arms } = &r.kind {
                if is_pure(&l, pure) && !arms.iter().any(|arm| arm_caps(arm, &l)) {
                    let out = arms
                        .iter()
                        .map(|a| Arm {
                            pat: a.pat.clone(),
                            span: a.span,
                            body: Expr {
                                span: sp,
                                kind: ExprKind::Bin {
                                    op,
                                    l: l.clone(),
                                    r: Box::new(a.body.clone()),
                                },
                            },
                        })
                        .collect();
                    *changed = true;
                    return Expr {
                        span: sp,
                        kind: ExprKind::Case {
                            scrut: scrut.clone(),
                            arms: out,
                        },
                    };
                }
            }
            Expr {
                span: sp,
                kind: ExprKind::Bin { op, l, r },
            }
        }
        other => Expr {
            span: sp,
            kind: other,
        },
    }
}

fn factor(e: Expr, pure: &HashSet<String>, changed: &mut bool) -> Expr {
    let sp = e.span;
    let ExprKind::Bin { op, l, r } = &e.kind else {
        return e;
    };
    if !matches!(op, BinOp::Add | BinOp::Sub) {
        return e;
    }
    fn mul_const(x: &Expr) -> Option<(Expr, u64)> {
        match &x.kind {
            ExprKind::Bin {
                op: BinOp::Mul,
                l,
                r,
            } => {
                if let Some(c) = as_int(r) {
                    Some(((**l).clone(), c))
                } else if let Some(c) = as_int(l) {
                    Some(((**r).clone(), c))
                } else {
                    None
                }
            }
            _ => None,
        }
    }
    let Some((a, ca)) = mul_const(l) else {
        return e;
    };
    let Some((b, cb)) = mul_const(r) else {
        return e;
    };
    if ca != cb || !is_pure(&a, pure) || !is_pure(&b, pure) || !is_int_expr(&a) || !is_int_expr(&b)
    {
        return e;
    }
    *changed = true;
    bin(BinOp::Mul, bin(*op, a, b, sp), int(ca), sp)
}

fn boolean(e: Expr, pure: &HashSet<String>, changed: &mut bool) -> Expr {
    let sp = e.span;
    let ExprKind::Bin { op, l, r } = &e.kind else {
        return e;
    };
    match op {
        BinOp::LAnd | BinOp::LOr => {
            if let (
                ExprKind::Bin {
                    op: lop,
                    l: al,
                    r: ar,
                },
                ExprKind::Bin {
                    op: rop,
                    l: bl,
                    r: br,
                },
            ) = (&l.kind, &r.kind)
            {
                if *op == BinOp::LOr
                    && *lop == BinOp::LAnd
                    && *rop == BinOp::LAnd
                    && same(al, bl)
                    && is_pure(al, pure)
                {
                    *changed = true;
                    return bin(
                        BinOp::LAnd,
                        (**al).clone(),
                        bin(BinOp::LOr, (**ar).clone(), (**br).clone(), sp),
                        sp,
                    );
                }
                if *op == BinOp::LAnd
                    && *lop == BinOp::LOr
                    && *rop == BinOp::LOr
                    && same(al, bl)
                    && is_pure(al, pure)
                {
                    *changed = true;
                    return bin(
                        BinOp::LOr,
                        (**al).clone(),
                        bin(BinOp::LAnd, (**ar).clone(), (**br).clone(), sp),
                        sp,
                    );
                }
            }
            if same(l, r) && is_pure(l, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return bin(BinOp::Ne, (**l).clone(), int(0), sp);
            }
        }
        BinOp::Eq | BinOp::Ne | BinOp::Lt | BinOp::Le | BinOp::Gt | BinOp::Ge => {
            if same(l, r) && is_pure(l, pure) && is_int_expr(l) {
                *changed = true;
                return int(match op {
                    BinOp::Eq | BinOp::Le | BinOp::Ge => 1,
                    _ => 0,
                });
            }
        }
        _ => {}
    }
    e
}

fn eta(e: Expr, pure: &HashSet<String>, changed: &mut bool) -> Expr {
    let ExprKind::Fn { params, body } = &e.kind else {
        return e;
    };
    if params.is_empty() || !distinct(params) {
        return e;
    }
    let ExprKind::App { fun, args } = &body.kind else {
        return e;
    };
    if args.len() != params.len() || !is_pure(fun, pure) {
        return e;
    }
    for (p, a) in params.iter().zip(args) {
        if !matches!(&a.kind, ExprKind::Var(n) if n == p) || count_var(fun, p) != 0 {
            return e;
        }
    }
    *changed = true;
    (**fun).clone()
}

fn cse(
    e: Expr,
    pure: &HashSet<String>,
    changed: &mut bool,
    used: &mut HashSet<String>,
    serial: &mut usize,
) -> Expr {
    let sp = e.span;
    let ExprKind::Bin { op, l, r } = &e.kind else {
        return e;
    };
    if matches!(op, BinOp::LAnd | BinOp::LOr) || !same(l, r) || !is_pure(l, pure) {
        return e;
    }
    if matches!(
        &l.kind,
        ExprKind::Var(_) | ExprKind::Int(_) | ExprKind::Float(_) | ExprKind::Str(_)
    ) {
        return e;
    }
    let name = fresh_opt_name(used, serial, "cse");
    let var1 = Expr {
        span: sp,
        kind: ExprKind::Var(name.clone()),
    };
    let var2 = Expr {
        span: sp,
        kind: ExprKind::Var(name.clone()),
    };
    let body = bin(*op, var1, var2, sp);
    *changed = true;
    Expr {
        span: sp,
        kind: ExprKind::Let {
            name,
            value: Box::new((**l).clone()),
            body: Box::new(body),
        },
    }
}

fn adv_expr(
    e: Expr,
    pure: &HashSet<String>,
    changed: &mut bool,
    used: &mut HashSet<String>,
    serial: &mut usize,
) -> Expr {
    let e = map_child(e, &mut |x| adv_expr(x, pure, changed, used, serial));
    let e = distribute(e, pure, changed);
    let e = factor(e, pure, changed);
    let e = boolean(e, pure, changed);
    let e = adv_un(e, pure, changed);
    let e = eta(e, pure, changed);
    let e = canon_assoc(e, pure, changed);
    cse(e, pure, changed, used, serial)
}

fn effect_const(e: Expr, n: u64, pure: &HashSet<String>) -> Expr {
    if is_pure(&e, pure) {
        int(n)
    } else {
        Expr {
            span: e.span,
            kind: ExprKind::Block {
                stmts: vec![e],
                tail: Box::new(int(n)),
            },
        }
    }
}

fn mega_arith(e: Expr, pure: &HashSet<String>, changed: &mut bool) -> Expr {
    let sp = e.span;
    let ExprKind::Bin { op, l, r } = &e.kind else {
        return e;
    };
    let l = (**l).clone();
    let r = (**r).clone();
    let li = as_int(&l);
    let ri = as_int(&r);
    use crate::BinOp::*;
    match op {
        Add => {
            if let ExprKind::Un {
                op: UnOp::Neg,
                e: x,
            } = &r.kind
            {
                *changed = true;
                return bin(Sub, l, (**x).clone(), sp);
            }
            if let ExprKind::Un {
                op: UnOp::Neg,
                e: x,
            } = &l.kind
            {
                if is_pure(x, pure) && is_pure(&r, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return bin(Sub, r, (**x).clone(), sp);
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Sub,
                l: x,
                r: y,
            } = &l.kind
            {
                if same(y, &r) && is_pure(y, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return (**x).clone();
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Sub,
                l: x,
                r: y,
            } = &r.kind
            {
                if same(&l, x) && is_pure(&l, pure) && is_pure(y, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return (**y).clone();
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Add,
                l: x,
                r: y,
            } = &l.kind
            {
                if let Some(a) = as_int(y) {
                    if let Some(b) = ri {
                        if ast_fold_add(a, b).is_some() {
                            *changed = true;
                            return bin(Add, (**x).clone(), int(a.wrapping_add(b)), sp);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Add,
                l: x,
                r: y,
            } = &r.kind
            {
                if let Some(a) = as_int(y) {
                    if let Some(b) = li {
                        if ast_fold_add(a, b).is_some() {
                            *changed = true;
                            return bin(Add, (**x).clone(), int(a.wrapping_add(b)), sp);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        Sub => {
            if li == Some(0) {
                *changed = true;
                return Expr {
                    span: sp,
                    kind: ExprKind::Un {
                        op: UnOp::Neg,
                        e: Box::new(r),
                    },
                };
            }
            if let ExprKind::Un {
                op: UnOp::Neg,
                e: x,
            } = &r.kind
            {
                *changed = true;
                return bin(Add, l, (**x).clone(), sp);
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Add,
                l: x,
                r: y,
            } = &l.kind
            {
                if same(y, &r) && is_pure(y, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return (**x).clone();
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Add,
                l: x,
                r: y,
            } = &r.kind
            {
                if same(&l, x) && is_pure(&l, pure) && is_pure(y, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return Expr {
                        span: sp,
                        kind: ExprKind::Un {
                            op: UnOp::Neg,
                            e: y.clone(),
                        },
                    };
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Sub,
                l: x,
                r: y,
            } = &l.kind
            {
                if same(y, &r) && is_pure(y, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return (**x).clone();
                }
            }
        }
        Mul => {
            if ri == Some(0) {
                *changed = true;
                return effect_const(l, 0, pure);
            }
            if li == Some(0) {
                *changed = true;
                return effect_const(r, 0, pure);
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Div,
                l: x,
                r: y,
            } = &l.kind
            {
                if same(x, &r) && as_int(y).is_some() && as_int(y) != Some(0) && is_pure(x, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return bin(Div, (**x).clone(), (**y).clone(), sp);
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Mul,
                l: x,
                r: y,
            } = &l.kind
            {
                if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (as_int(y), ri) {
                    if ast_fold_mul(a, b).is_some() {
                        *changed = true;
                        return bin(Mul, (**x).clone(), int(a.wrapping_mul(b)), sp);
                    }
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Mul,
                l: x,
                r: y,
            } = &r.kind
            {
                if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (as_int(y), li) {
                    if ast_fold_mul(a, b).is_some() {
                        *changed = true;
                        return bin(Mul, (**x).clone(), int(a.wrapping_mul(b)), sp);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        Div => {
            if ri == Some(1) {
                *changed = true;
                return l;
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Div,
                l: x,
                r: c1,
            } = &l.kind
            {
                if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (as_int(c1), ri) {
                    if a != 0 && b != 0 {
                        if let Some(product) = ast_fold_mul(a, b) {
                            *changed = true;
                            return bin(Div, (**x).clone(), int(product), sp);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        Rem => {
            if ri == Some(1) {
                *changed = true;
                return effect_const(l, 0, pure);
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Rem,
                l: x,
                r: c1,
            } = &l.kind
            {
                if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (as_int(c1), ri) {
                    if a == b && b != 0 {
                        *changed = true;
                        return bin(Rem, (**x).clone(), int(b), sp);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        _ => {}
    }
    e
}

fn mega_bits(e: Expr, pure: &HashSet<String>, changed: &mut bool) -> Expr {
    let sp = e.span;
    let ExprKind::Bin { op, l, r } = &e.kind else {
        return e;
    };
    let l = (**l).clone();
    let r = (**r).clone();
    let li = as_int(&l);
    let ri = as_int(&r);
    use crate::BinOp::*;
    match op {
        And => {
            if ri == Some(0) {
                *changed = true;
                return effect_const(l, 0, pure);
            }
            if li == Some(0) {
                *changed = true;
                return effect_const(r, 0, pure);
            }
            if ri == Some(u64::MAX) {
                *changed = true;
                return l;
            }
            if li == Some(u64::MAX) {
                *changed = true;
                return r;
            }
            if same(&l, &r) && is_pure(&l, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return l;
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin { op: Or, l: x, r: y } = &l.kind {
                if same(x, &r) && is_pure(x, pure) && is_pure(y, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return r;
                }
                if same(y, &r) && is_pure(y, pure) && is_pure(x, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return r;
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin { op: Or, l: x, r: m } = &r.kind {
                if same(x, &l) && is_pure(x, pure) && is_pure(m, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return l;
                }
                if same(m, &l) && is_pure(m, pure) && is_pure(x, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return l;
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Xor,
                l: x,
                r: y,
            } = &r.kind
            {
                if same(&l, x) && is_pure(&l, pure) && is_pure(y, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return bin(
                        And,
                        l,
                        Expr {
                            span: sp,
                            kind: ExprKind::Un {
                                op: UnOp::BitNot,
                                e: y.clone(),
                            },
                        },
                        sp,
                    );
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: And,
                l: x,
                r: y,
            } = &l.kind
            {
                if same(y, &r) && is_pure(y, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return bin(And, (**x).clone(), (**y).clone(), sp);
                }
            }
            if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (li, ri) {
                if let Some(value) = ast_fold_bitwise(And, a, b) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return int(value);
                }
            }
        }
        Or => {
            if ri == Some(0) {
                *changed = true;
                return l;
            }
            if li == Some(0) {
                *changed = true;
                return r;
            }
            if ri == Some(u64::MAX) {
                *changed = true;
                return effect_const(l, u64::MAX, pure);
            }
            if li == Some(u64::MAX) {
                *changed = true;
                return effect_const(r, u64::MAX, pure);
            }
            if same(&l, &r) && is_pure(&l, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return l;
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: And,
                l: x,
                r: m,
            } = &r.kind
            {
                if same(x, &l) && is_pure(x, pure) && is_pure(m, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return l;
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Xor,
                l: x,
                r: m,
            } = &r.kind
            {
                if same(x, &l) && is_pure(x, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return bin(Or, l, (**m).clone(), sp);
                }
            }
            if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (li, ri) {
                if let Some(value) = ast_fold_bitwise(Or, a, b) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return int(value);
                }
            }
        }
        Xor => {
            if ri == Some(0) {
                *changed = true;
                return l;
            }
            if li == Some(0) {
                *changed = true;
                return r;
            }
            if ri == Some(u64::MAX) {
                *changed = true;
                return Expr {
                    span: sp,
                    kind: ExprKind::Un {
                        op: UnOp::BitNot,
                        e: Box::new(l),
                    },
                };
            }
            if li == Some(u64::MAX) {
                *changed = true;
                return Expr {
                    span: sp,
                    kind: ExprKind::Un {
                        op: UnOp::BitNot,
                        e: Box::new(r),
                    },
                };
            }
            if same(&l, &r) && is_pure(&l, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return int(0);
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Xor,
                l: x,
                r: y,
            } = &r.kind
            {
                if same(&l, x) && is_pure(&l, pure) && is_pure(y, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return (**y).clone();
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: Xor,
                l: x,
                r: y,
            } = &l.kind
            {
                if same(y, &r) && is_pure(&r, pure) && is_pure(x, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return (**x).clone();
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin { op: Or, l: x, r: m } = &l.kind {
                if same(x, &r) && is_pure(x, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return bin(
                        And,
                        Expr {
                            span: sp,
                            kind: ExprKind::Un {
                                op: UnOp::BitNot,
                                e: x.clone(),
                            },
                        },
                        (**m).clone(),
                        sp,
                    );
                }
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: And,
                l: x,
                r: m,
            } = &r.kind
            {
                if same(&l, x) && is_pure(x, pure) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return bin(
                        And,
                        l,
                        Expr {
                            span: sp,
                            kind: ExprKind::Un {
                                op: UnOp::BitNot,
                                e: m.clone(),
                            },
                        },
                        sp,
                    );
                }
            }
            if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (li, ri) {
                if a <= AST_INT_FOLD_MAX && b <= AST_INT_FOLD_MAX {
                    *changed = true;
                    return int(a ^ b);
                }
            }
        }
        Shl | Shr => {
            if ri == Some(0) {
                *changed = true;
                return l;
            }
            if let ExprKind::Bin {
                op: iop,
                l: x,
                r: a,
            } = &l.kind
            {
                if *iop == *op {
                    if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (as_int(a), ri) {
                        *changed = true;
                        return bin(*op, (**x).clone(), int(a.wrapping_add(b) & 63), sp);
                    }
                }
            }
            if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (li, ri) {
                if let Some(value) = eval_bin(*op, a, b) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return int(value);
                }
            }
        }
        _ => {}
    }
    e
}

fn mega_logic(e: Expr, pure: &HashSet<String>, changed: &mut bool) -> Expr {
    let sp = e.span;
    let ExprKind::Bin { op, l, r } = &e.kind else {
        return e;
    };
    let l = (**l).clone();
    let r = (**r).clone();
    let li = as_int(&l);
    let ri = as_int(&r);
    use crate::BinOp::*;
    match op {
        LAnd => {
            if li == Some(0) {
                *changed = true;
                return int(0);
            }
            if ri == Some(0) {
                *changed = true;
                return effect_const(l, 0, pure);
            }
            if li == Some(1) {
                *changed = true;
                return bin(Ne, r, int(0), sp);
            }
            if ri == Some(1) {
                *changed = true;
                return bin(Ne, l, int(0), sp);
            }
            if same(&l, &r) && is_pure(&l, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return bin(Ne, l, int(0), sp);
            }
        }
        LOr => {
            if li == Some(1) {
                *changed = true;
                return int(1);
            }
            if ri == Some(1) {
                *changed = true;
                return effect_const(l, 1, pure);
            }
            if li == Some(0) {
                *changed = true;
                return bin(Ne, r, int(0), sp);
            }
            if ri == Some(0) {
                *changed = true;
                return bin(Ne, l, int(0), sp);
            }
            if same(&l, &r) && is_pure(&l, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return bin(Ne, l, int(0), sp);
            }
        }
        _ => {}
    }
    e
}

fn mega_cmp(e: Expr, pure: &HashSet<String>, changed: &mut bool) -> Expr {
    let sp = e.span;
    let ExprKind::Bin { op, l, r } = &e.kind else {
        return e;
    };
    let l = (**l).clone();
    let r = (**r).clone();
    let li = as_int(&l);
    let ri = as_int(&r);
    use crate::BinOp::*;
    match op {
        Eq | Ne | Lt | Gt | Le | Ge => {
            if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (li, ri) {
                if let Some(value) = eval_bin(*op, a, b) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return int(value);
                }
            }
            if same(&l, &r) && is_pure(&l, pure) {
                *changed = true;
                return int(match op {
                    Eq | Le | Ge => 1,
                    _ => 0,
                });
            }
            if is_bool(&l) {
                if ri == Some(0) {
                    *changed = true;
                    return if matches!(op, Eq) {
                        Expr {
                            span: sp,
                            kind: ExprKind::Un {
                                op: UnOp::Not,
                                e: Box::new(l),
                            },
                        }
                    } else if matches!(op, Ne) {
                        l
